    10	fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
    11	    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
    12	    start+text[start..].chars().take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>()
    13	}
    14	fn line_span(file: &str, text: &str, site: &crate::Site) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
    15	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
    16	    let at = tokens.iter().position(|t| t.site == *site)?;
    17	    let end = tokens[at..].iter().find(|t| t.kind == Kind::Newline)?;
    18	    Some((
    19	        offset(text, site.line, 1),
    20	        offset(text, end.site.line, end.site.col),
    21	    ))
    22	}
    23	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize, String) {
    24	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    25	    let mut end = text.len();
    26	    for (next, s) in text.lines().enumerate().skip(line as usize) {
    27	        if !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--") {
    28	            end = offset(text, next as u32 + 1, 1);
    29	            break;
    30	        }
    31	    }
    32	    (start, end, text[start..end].to_owned())
    33	}

   400	}
   401	
   402	fn current_default(file:&str,text:&str,old:&ast::ColumnDecl,new:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize,String)> {
   403	    let authored=|column:&ast::ColumnDecl|column.default.as_ref().map(print::expression);
   404	    if authored(old)==authored(new){return None;}
   405	    let replacement=authored(new).map(|value|format!("default {value}")).unwrap_or_default();
   406	    if let Some(value)=&old.default {
   407	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);
   408	        let at=tokens.iter().position(|token|token.site==*value.site())?;
   409	        let keyword=at.checked_sub(1).filter(|i|tokens[*i].value=="default")?;
   410	        let end=at+if matches!(value,ast::Expr::Unary{..}){2}else{1};
   411	        let end=offset(text,tokens.get(end)?.site.line,tokens.get(end)?.site.col).min(text.len());
   412	        Some((offset(text,tokens[keyword].site.line,tokens[keyword].site.col),text[..end].trim_end().len(),replacement))
   413	    }else{
   414	        let(_,end)=super::release_bridge::type_span(file,text,old)?;
   415	        Some((end,end,format!(" {replacement}")))
   416	    }
   417	}
   418	
   419	pub(super) fn input_accepts(old: &ast::ScalarType, new: &ast::ScalarType) -> bool {
   420	    if old == new {
   421	        return true;
   422	    }
   423	    if old.optional && !new.optional {
   424	        return false;
   425	    }
   426	    use ast::TypeKind::*;
   427	    let bounds = |k: &ast::TypeKind| match k {
   428	        Int => Some((i64::MIN, i64::MAX)),
   429	        IntRange { min, max } => Some((min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN), max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX))),
   430	        _ => None,
   431	    };
   432	    match (bounds(&old.kind), bounds(&new.kind)) {
   433	        (Some((a, b)), Some((x, y))) => x <= a && y >= b,
   434	        _ => match (&old.kind,&new.kind){(Enum{variants:a},Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),_=>old.kind==new.kind},
   435	    }
   436	}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.extractions) {
        let extraction=snapback4_core::schema::Extraction{child:d.child.clone(),parent:d.parent.clone(),reference:d.reference.clone(),fields:d.fields.iter().cloned().collect()};
        schema.extractions.push(extraction);
    }
    let mut forward_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut forward_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.backfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);}
    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);}
    for table in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
        if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {
            if old==&table.name || catalog.tables.contains_key(old) || schema.table_renames.values().any(|v|v==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&table.site,"table rename requires one distinct predecessor-only source","keep one name for each row lifetime"));
            } else {schema.table_renames.insert(table.name.clone(),old.clone());}
        }
        for column in table.columns.iter().filter(|c|c.renamed_from.is_some()) {
            let old=column.renamed_from.as_ref().unwrap();
            if old==&column.name || table.columns.iter().any(|c|&c.name==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("rename source {}.{old} must be a distinct predecessor-only column",table.name),"remove the current old column and retain only the renamed declaration"));continue
            }
            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {
                schema.asset_renames.entry(table.name.clone()).or_default().insert(column.name.clone(),old.clone());continue;
            }
            if matches!(&column.type_.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref{..}|ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}|ast::TypeKind::List{..})
                || column.maintained.is_some() || column.sequence.is_some() || column.written_by.is_some()
            {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("column rename on {}.{} requires an authoritative scalar without reference, asset, collection, captured or lifetime ownership",table.name,column.name),"use a bridge migration that preserves the protected field's authority and lifetime"));continue
            }
            let declaration=|input:&str,output:&str,prefix:&str|ast::BackfillDecl{
                table:table.name.clone(),name:format!("$rename:{prefix}:{old}:{}",column.name),
                args:vec![ast::ArgDecl{alias:None,default:None,deprecated:false,name:input.into(),type_:column.type_.clone(),site:column.site.clone()}],
                value:ast::Expr::Object{fields:vec![ast::ObjectField{spread:false,name:output.into(),value:Some(ast::Expr::Name{name:input.into(),site:column.site.clone()}),site:column.site.clone()}],site:column.site.clone()},site:column.site.clone()
            };
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(old,&column.name,"up"),false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(&column.name,old,"down"),true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);
        }
    }
}

//! The catalog: every table (declared or generated for a maintain) with its
//! columns and indexes, plus the named shapes and functions, and where each
//! schema path was declared so a core validation issue can point at a line.

use crate::ast::*;
use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Site, name_rewrite, unknown};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TableInfo {
    pub through: Option<snapback4_core::schema::through::Through>,
    pub captures: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub recorded: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub decl: TableDecl,
    pub columns: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub indexes: Vec<IndexDecl>,
    /// Set when the table is a maintain's generated target.
    pub maintained: Option<MaintainDecl>,
}

impl TableInfo {
    pub fn total_unique(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.iter().all(|column| column == "id" || self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — a scan cursor carries declared components only;
    // uniqueness with a nullable component cannot resolve ties between nulls.
    pub fn total_cursor(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id") || index.unique && index.words.is_none()
            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| match index.computed.get(column) {
                Some(expr) => super::time_indexes::key(&self.decl.name, &self.columns, column, expr).is_ok(),
                None => self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional),
            })
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Catalog {
    pub ordinary_searches: Vec<String>,
    pub source: crate::source::SourceMap,
    pub feed_views: BTreeMap<String, super::feed_view::Plan>,
    pub extremum_domains: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    pub read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub canonical_read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub view_reads: BTreeMap<String, crate::ast::MergeExpr>,
    pub view_types: BTreeMap<String, crate::types::Ty>,
    pub planner_notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    /// Exact cap declarations that would admit an otherwise fully verified
    /// owner-or-membership page, keyed by the read's source site.
    pub membership_cap_repairs: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub synthesized: std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
    pub documents: Vec<Document>,
    pub tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>,
    pub maintains: BTreeMap<String, MaintainDecl>,
    pub shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl>,
    pub functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>,
    pub effects: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

impl Catalog {
    pub fn build(documents: Vec<Document>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Self {
        let mut tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut maintains = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effects = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effect_sites: BTreeMap<String, Site> = BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in &documents {
            for table in &doc.tables {
                if tables.contains_key(&table.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&table.site, format!("duplicate table {}", table.name), "rename one of the two tables"));
                    continue;
                }
                let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
                let mut ok = true;
                for column in &table.columns {
                    if column.name == "id" {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("table {} gets id implicitly; remove the id column", table.name), "delete this line"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if columns.insert(column.name.clone(), column.type_.clone()).is_some() {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("duplicate column {}.{}", table.name, column.name), "rename one of the two columns"));
                        ok = false;
                    }
                }
                for field in &table.search {
                    if !matches!(columns.get(field), Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. })) {
                        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&table.site, format!("search {} requires a text or enum column on {}", field, table.name), format!("declare `{field}: text <=2000` or `{field}: enum('one', 'two')` before `search {field}`")).guide("search"));
                    }

- Add `customer: principal renamed from owner` to current `notes`.
- Widen current `n` to `int ?`.
- Change current `byCount` to `count, customer, id`.

The current query remains `notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount`.

The selective bridge retains the predecessor spelling `owner`, adds `count`, and reuses the current query. It then copies `by byCount: count, customer, id`, producing an unknown-column compilation failure: `customer` is absent from the generated table. Both complete-current fallbacks fail because current source lacks predecessor table `retired`.

This is a transportable plain-column identity change, not an index-meaning change requiring current context. The index should use the injectively aligned component `owner` in this generated view. The comparison-only alignment in `release_bridge_indexes.rs` cannot repair the copied declaration.
